============================================================
7. DECISION CHECKLIST
============================================================

We are checking ONE thing:

[ ] Does the upload route automatically create orchestration jobs?
[ ] Does the website/domain route automatically create orchestration jobs?
[ ] Do those jobs chain into UDUC / Website Unified Content?
[ ] Do those jobs chain into UUCD?
[ ] Do those jobs chain into Body Store?
[ ] Do those jobs chain into Certification?

Decision:
- PATCH ROUTES if routes do not call orchestration.
- PATCH CHAIN if routes call orchestration but chain stops downstream.
- APPROVE if both routes already trigger the full pipeline.
